## Continuation instructions for scheduled sessions

Before any batch, obtain a fresh actual app quota reading and UTC time; update runs/offline/tomato_triangle_withdrawal_01/quota_lease.json using those readings (never an assumed/future timestamp). The batch launcher refuses leases older than 480 seconds, any reported bucket at >=45% used, and work within 65 seconds of the deadline. Each worker/audit is capped and runs serially under the original shared lock; the parent also holds logs/offline_overnight.lock. Inspect all withdrawal_batch*/status.json and any live worker before resuming. Use fresh directories, at most 12 mechanical calls per batch, <=0.125 mm nominal withdrawal steps. Accept only endpoints with passing independent audit. The earliest unpredicted attempt is a retained optimizer failure, not evidence of mechanical collapse; the signed secant predictor provides only an uncommitted position guess.

The latest independent audits may take tens of seconds under the unchanged CPU cap. If an audit times out, retain the endpoint as unaudited and re-audit before claiming progress; never advance from it. If withdrawal, energy or stability checks fail, diagnose/refine numerical continuation within existing gates; do not increase insertion depth to evade the failure. After full withdrawal, develop separately versioned supported compression/reopening/reclosure checks with an explicit support-work ledger and no support engagement displacement. Existing qualified source files must remain immutable; new numerical versions need an explicit verified provenance transition. Only after this gate passes continue deeper insertion from the original insertion checkpoint with original histories intact.

Run the 111-test regression suite via the new cohort run_tests.py once no physics worker is active. Run qualify_progress.py only after the current batch finishes. This verifies all 241 pre-existing source/document hashes, saved state/source hashes, finite metrics, independent audits, fixed worker caps, and exact damage/history retention from the 8.125 mm seed. Qualifier outputs qualification.json; update this journal and handoff.json with actual results. No full-cut or release claim until every physical gate is qualified.
